### Correction/confirmation block — foilbox run618 late browser handoff (~08:05 CDT)
The silent browser report landed after close-out. It CONFIRMS (not corrects) the no-claim call on CCFF00 RUBIK: the holder-phase mint runs through OpenSea's native mint widget at opensea.io/collection/ccff00-rubik, with eligibility enforced offchain by OpenSea's backend (4,134-wallet allowlist) and an EIP-712 signature issued only after connecting a wallet in the widget — there is no standalone public eligibility checker or headless signature endpoint. The drop was never headless-fireable (Foil's wallet is Bankr-provisioned and in-browser wallet connects are a hard no). Route verdict: likely genuine (exact-collection widget, SeaDrop-clone contract, holder base matches CCFF00) but never conclusively tied — hoodstreet.capital had no RUBIK page and none of the official X accounts announced it during the window. Pipeline entry updated to ended (unclaimed).
